-- Table 1: media_analytics_display
DESCRIBE TABLE prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_display;
output:
col_name	data_type	comment
Date	date	Date of the customer engagement (Impressions / Click), or matched conversion (Meta only). Most platforms report on matched conversions as per the date of the click or impression, while Meta reflects the date of the conversion, not aligned to the engagement date.
Week_Start_Mon	date	The date of the start of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Week_End_Sun	date	The date of the end of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Week_End_Sat	date	The date of the end of the week, with a Sunday through Monday week (standard calendar week).
Agency	varchar(25)	The name of the Agency who activated the record.
Audience	string	Audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy intended to define sub segments such as Military, 55+, etc.
Audience_Descriptor	varchar(155)	The targeted audience, as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy
Benefitting_Segment	varchar(155)	Benefitting Segment as per creative / ad taxonomy Intended to denote what lob / brand / product will benefit from the ad, regardless of what budget is came from (Master Brand or Budget Source).
Brand	varchar(25)	The T-Mobile Brand. A high level categorization of Postpaid, Prepaid, TFB, Broadband, Financial Services, etc. Not Specified is where the details within the record do not have enough indicators to identify a Brand. Other falls outside of the primary Brand we track (organic social or other specialized services).
LOB	varchar(25)	T-Mobile Line of Business that falls under a LOB, such as Metro, T-Mobile Prepaid, Beyond the Smartphone (BTS), etc. Not Specified are missing enough indicators to assign an LOB. Other falls outside of standard LOBs.
Channel_Abbr	varchar(25)	An abbreviated code to reflect the detail-level Channel definition. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Name	varchar(35)	The detail-level channel name that reflects the media channel for the record. (Online Video, OTT, Linear TV, Paid Social - LinkedIn, Paid Social - Snapchat, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Abbr	varchar(35)	An abbreviated code to reflect a channel grouping. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Name	varchar(35)	The Channel Group Name to reflect the media channel, but aggregated to logic grouping (such as Paid Social, Paid Search, Display, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Media_Tactic	varchar(35)	The Media Tactic of the record. Derived from a combination from Channel assignment, and other considerations such as Agency for Display - Direct, and Display - Programmatic. Or designations of Paid Search based on the search type - such as Paid Search - Branded, Paid Search - Non-branded. Primarily used to align MA Integrated to MMM, PIE, T&L, and other media analytics resources for consistent data alignment.
Funnel_Stage	varchar(50)	The Funnel Stage the ad is intended to target. Based on sub program, optimization metric, or other indicators. For TFB, The Funnel Stage matches the Sub Program from campaign taxonomy.
Publisher_Platform	string	The platform used by the publisher.
iSpot_Pixel_Status	string	iSpot Only - used to identify untagged placements in the existing iSpot data pipeline
iSpot_Server_to_Server_Status	string	iSpot only - used to identify untagged placements in the existing iSpot data pipeline
Parent_Company	string	The parent company associated with the record.
Account_ID	varchar(15)	The unique account id as it exists on the source platform.
Account_Name	varchar(100)	The name of the account, as it exists on the source platform.
Campaign_ID	varchar(75)	The unique campaign ID, as it exists on the source platform. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most purposes, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Campaign_Name	varchar(205)	The campaign name, as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most conversations, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Placement_ID	varchar(15)	Unique identifier for the placement-level entity on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Placement_Name	varchar(355)	The Placement Name as it exists on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Creative_ID	varchar(100)	The unique Creative ID as it exists on the source platform.
Creative_Name	varchar(355)	The Creative Name as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. This name does not reflect a description of the asset, as it is a container at the creative level that can support multiple assets (such as carousel). This is the level that the messaging campaign exists at, but messaging campaign is better served via TMM Campaign Desc.
Campaign_Year	varchar(55)	The year of the campaign (related to campaign id, not messaging campaign), as it appears in the taxonomy.
Budget_Source	string	Budget Source as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Budgeted_Product_Line	varchar(55)	Budgeted Product Line as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Master_Brand	varchar(55)	Master Brand as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
OEM_Funded	varchar(55)	Original Equipment Manufacturer (OEM) Funded indicator as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Program	string	Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Sub_Program	string	Sub-Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Primary_Language	varchar(55)	Primary Language as is provided by campaign taxonomy. Note that there is a better field for the messaging language, derived from creative taxonomy or other record markers. See Creative_Language.
Optimization_Metric	varchar(55)	The engagement metric that the ad is intended to drive (mid-funnel, SEC, etc.). As per campaign taxonomy, and may not reflect what campaign configuration option was selected on the platform when the campaign was placed.
Measurement_Metric	varchar(50)	An indicator for the primary matched conversion metric to be used for performance analysis. Derived from Optimization metric from campaign taxonomy, or other markers in the record.
Buy_Type	string	The Buy Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Platform	varchar(55)	The designated Platform, we per the campaign taxonomy.
Telco_Profile	varchar(55)	Telco Profile as per campaign taxonomy. May not reflect actual campaign configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Campaign_Objective	varchar(55)	"The targeted matched conversion funnel level for the placement, as per placement taxonomy. (Awareness, Consideration, Choice, etc.) OR the conversion metric. 
This is duplicative of Optimization_Metric, which is better populated. "
Ad_Device_Targeting	varchar(55)	The targeted device type for an ad, as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy
Buying_Platform	varchar(55)	Buying Platform as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Data_Type	varchar(55)	Data Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Device	varchar(55)	"The Device type the ad was interacted with, as per the cm360 record. (Web, App, Desktop, etc.)
Display (cm360) Only."
Enterprise_Base	varchar(55)	Enterprise or Base targeting as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
LOB_Base	varchar(55)	LOB_Base as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Publisher	varchar(55)	Publisher info as for display, placement taxonomy, iSpot TV details, or Affiliate Program. Not applicable for other channels.
Target_Age	varchar(55)	Target Age audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Target_Behavior	varchar(55)	Target Behavior as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Geo_Type	varchar(55)	"Indicates the Geo Type, National or Local for the record. Derived from markers within the record such as campaign name, or other indicators.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Geo	varchar(155)	"Geo-based audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Occupation	varchar(55)	Targeted Occupation audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Targeting_Tactics	varchar(55)	Targeting Tactics as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Test_and_Learn	varchar(155)	Indicator if the row is apart of a Test and Learn conversion lift studied as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. Not well populated, not a reliable indicator for Test and Learn participation.
Video_Type	varchar(55)	"The video type, as per the placement taxonomy.
Display Only."
Asset_Size	varchar(155)	The Asset Size of the creative, as per the creative / ad taxonomy.
Benefitting_LOB	varchar(155)	"Benefitting LOB as per creative / ad taxonomy Intended to denote what lob / brand / product will benefit from the ad, regardless of what budget is came from (Master Brand or Budget Source).
Used in deriving the LOB definitions."
Creative_Agency	varchar(155)	Creative Agency as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Agency_Ad_Number	varchar(155)	Creative Agency Ad Number as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Language	string	Creative Language as per creative / ad taxonomy, enhanced by more robust marker indicators within the creative, placement, or campaign details.
Creative_Message_Variation	string	The creative message variation as per the creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Version	varchar(155)	Creative Version as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Duration	varchar(35)	Duration of the ad (if video) as per creative / ad taxonomy.
File_Format_Type	varchar(55)	The file format of the asset, as per the creative / ad taxonomy.
Media_Format	varchar(55)	Media format as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Type	string	"The type of the creative asset (cm360), or the Affiliate Ad Type (affiliate). Does not reflect the asset info from creative / ad taxonomy.
Display (cm360) and Affiliate only."
Call_to_Action	varchar(155)	null
Media_Creative_Message	varchar(155)	The Creative Message as per the creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Intent	varchar(155)	An indicator as to what the creative asset was to message the customer. Often populated with funnel description (Awareness, consideration, etc.) or a program indicator (BOAT, BOWLING, DINNER, etc.).
Creative_Size	varchar(255)	"The size of the creative asset, as per cm360. Does not reflect the asset info from creative / ad taxonomy.
Display (cm360) only."
Site_Directory	varchar(255)	A grouping of Site names, to a more aggregated level. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_ID	varchar(35)	The ID associated with the Site. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_Name	varchar(255)	The name of the site the as appeared on. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager. Not comprehensive as some display is sourced from Omnicom direct buys, or via DSPs which may obfuscate the Site name.
Spend_Source	varchar(55)	An indicator as to where the spend value was sourced from. Primarily used to identify which DSP provided the source data for the placement, spend data. Note that spend at a creative level is often derived from a placement-level spend, allocated to creative by weighted impressions.
TMM_Code	varchar(25)	The TMM Campaign Code, primarily derived from creative name, but supplemented by searching in other fields including the destination URL.
TMM_Campaign_Desc	string	The Description of the TMM Messaging Campaign. The primary resource for identifying which messaging campaign the ad supports.  Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Category	string	A categorization of the TMM Code. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Message	string	Categorization of TMM campaigns to designate device, service, or other intended offer designations. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Campaign_Group	varchar(155)	The Campaign Group description, as defined by the TMM source. (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
Conversions_Flag	string	An indicator if the record is expected to have Matched Conversion volumes or not. Intended to be used for filtering results when analyzing ad performance that is not expected to have matched conversions. The lack of matched conversions is due to the record source not having a data sharing agreement with T-Mobile (such as TikTok), or from a non-clickable media such as Content Syndication or OOH.
MDF	string	Manufacturer Device Fund - indicates if the record has been flagged as promoting a specific manufacturer initiative, such as iPhone, Samsung, Netflix, etc. Can support multiple MDFs in a single record, separated by comma. Derived from markers in the TMM Campaign Desc, and select values from Creative or Placement.
Clicks	bigint	The number of users who clicked on the ad (only viable for clickable media) As determined by the source platform.
Impressions	bigint	The number of users who have been served an ad, and viewed it. As determined by the source platform. For Linear TV, this is the extrapolated Impression volume, and not the strict panel volume.
Spend	double	The cost for the ad. May not include Fees or taxes.
Video_Starts	bigint	"The number of engagement that began watching a video (OLV).
Only available on limited platforms."
Video_Played_at_25perc	bigint	The number of engagements where they watched a minimum of 25% of the length of the video (OLV).
Video_Played_at_50perc	bigint	The number of engagements where they watched a minimum of 50% of the length of the video (OLV).
Video_Played_at_75perc	bigint	The number of engagements where they watched a minimum of 75% of the length of the video (OLV).
Video_Played_at_100perc	bigint	The number of engagements where they watched the entirety (100%) of a video (OLV).
BTS_Quality_Traffic	bigint	Web event attributed (last touch) to served advertisement from the platform. See the Site Metrics Directory SharePoint doc for metric definition details.

-- Table 2: media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
DESCRIBE TABLE prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot;
output:
col_name	data_type	comment
view_source	string	The Lab42 Media Analytics view where the source record can be found. For the Integrated view, some records are limited from the source views to ensure deduplication of records (such as OTT is from iSpot, and the Display record is suppressed).
Date	date	Date of the customer engagement (Impressions / Click), or matched conversion (Meta only). Most platforms report on matched conversions as per the date of the click or impression, while Meta reflects the date of the conversion, not aligned to the engagement date.
Week_Start_Mon	date	The date of the start of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Week_End_Sun	date	The date of the end of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Agency	string	The name of the Agency who activated the record.
Brand	string	The T-Mobile Brand. A high level categorization of Postpaid, Prepaid, TFB, Broadband, Financial Services, etc. Not Specified is where the details within the record do not have enough indicators to identify a Brand. Other falls outside of the primary Brand we track (organic social or other specialized services).
LOB	string	T-Mobile Line of Business that falls under a LOB, such as Metro, T-Mobile Prepaid, Beyond the Smartphone (BTS), etc. Not Specified are missing enough indicators to assign an LOB. Other falls outside of standard LOBs.
Account_ID	string	The unique account id as it exists on the source platform.
Account_Name	string	The name of the account, as it exists on the source platform.
Account_Type	string	The type of the account (Microsoft, Google, etc.). Used exclusively for Paid Search.
Campaign_ID	string	The unique campaign ID, as it exists on the source platform. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most purposes, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Campaign_Name	string	The campaign name, as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most conversations, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Campaign_Year	string	The year of the campaign (related to campaign id, not messaging campaign), as it appears in the taxonomy.
Placement_ID	string	Unique identifier for the placement-level entity on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Placement_Name	string	The Placement Name as it exists on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Creative_ID	string	The unique Creative ID as it exists on the source platform.
Creative_Name	string	The Creative Name as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. This name does not reflect a description of the asset, as it is a container at the creative level that can support multiple assets (such as carousel). This is the level that the messaging campaign exists at, but messaging campaign is better served via TMM Campaign Desc.
Search_Campaign_Category	string	A categorization for the Campaign as defined in Paid Search.
Budget_Source	string	Budget Source as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Budgeted_Product_Line	string	Budgeted Product Line as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Master_Brand	string	Master Brand as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
OEM_Funded	string	Original Equipment Manufacturer (OEM) Funded indicator as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Program	string	Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Sub_Program	string	Sub-Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Primary_Language	string	Primary Language as is provided by campaign taxonomy. Note that there is a better field for the messaging language, derived from creative taxonomy or other record markers. See Creative_Language.
Channel_Abbr	string	An abbreviated code to reflect the detail-level Channel definition. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Name	string	The detail-level channel name that reflects the media channel for the record. (Online Video, OTT, Linear TV, Paid Social - LinkedIn, Paid Social - Snapchat, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Abbr	string	An abbreviated code to reflect a channel grouping. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Name	string	The Channel Group Name to reflect the media channel, but aggregated to logic grouping (such as Paid Social, Paid Search, Display, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Media_Tactic	string	The Media Tactic of the record. Derived from a combination from Channel assignment, and other considerations such as Agency for Display - Direct, and Display - Programmatic. Or designations of Paid Search based on the search type - such as Paid Search - Branded, Paid Search - Non-branded. Primarily used to align MA Integrated to MMM, PIE, T&L, and other media analytics resources for consistent data alignment.
Optimization_Metric	string	The engagement metric that the ad is intended to drive (mid-funnel, SEC, etc.). As per campaign taxonomy, and may not reflect what campaign configuration option was selected on the platform when the campaign was placed.
Buy_Type	string	The Buy Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Telco_Profile	string	Telco Profile as per campaign taxonomy. May not reflect actual campaign configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Buying_Platform	string	Buying Platform as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Data_Type	string	Data Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Enterprise_Base	string	Enterprise or Base targeting as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Publisher	string	Publisher info as for display, placement taxonomy, iSpot TV details, or Affiliate Program. Not applicable for other channels.
Target_Age	string	Target Age audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Geo_Type	string	"Indicates the Geo Type, National or Local for the record. Derived from markers within the record such as campaign name, or other indicators.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Geo	string	"Geo-based audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Occupation	string	Targeted Occupation audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Test_and_Learn	string	Indicator if the row is apart of a Test and Learn conversion lift studied as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. Not well populated, not a reliable indicator for Test and Learn participation.
Creative_Agency	string	Creative Agency as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Agency_Ad_Number	string	Creative Agency Ad Number as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Language	string	Creative Language as per creative / ad taxonomy, enhanced by more robust marker indicators within the creative, placement, or campaign details.
Creative_Version	string	Creative Version as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Duration	string	Duration of the ad (if video) as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Media_Format	string	Media format as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Funnel_Stage	string	The Funnel Stage the ad is intended to target. Based on sub program, optimization metric, or other indicators. For TFB, The Funnel Stage matches the Sub Program from campaign taxonomy.
Audience	string	Audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy intended to define sub segments such as Military, 55+, etc.
Audience_Descriptor	string	The targeted audience, as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy
Benefitting_Segment	string	Benefitting Segment as per creative / ad taxonomy Intended to denote what lob / brand / product will benefit from the ad, regardless of what budget is came from (Master Brand or Budget Source).
Creative_Intent	string	An indicator as to what the creative asset was to message the customer. Often populated with funnel description (Awareness, consideration, etc.) or a program indicator (BOAT, BOWLING, DINNER, etc.).
Spend_Source	string	An indicator as to where the spend value was sourced from. Primarily used to identify which DSP provided the source data for the placement, spend data. Note that spend at a creative level is often derived from a placement-level spend, allocated to creative by weighted impressions.
TMM_Code	string	The TMM Campaign Code, primarily derived from creative name, but supplemented by searching in other fields including the destination URL.
TMM_Campaign_Desc	string	The Description of the TMM Messaging Campaign. The primary resource for identifying which messaging campaign the ad supports.  Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Category	string	A categorization of the TMM Code. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Message	string	Categorization of TMM campaigns to designate device, service, or other intended offer designations. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
Site_Directory	string	A grouping of Site names, to a more aggregated level. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_ID	string	The ID associated with the Site. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_Name	string	The name of the site the as appeared on. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager. Not comprehensive as some display is sourced from Omnicom direct buys, or via DSPs which may obfuscate the Site name.
Conversions_Flag	string	An indicator if the record is expected to have Matched Conversion volumes or not. Intended to be used for filtering results when analyzing ad performance that is not expected to have matched conversions. The lack of matched conversions is due to the record source not having a data sharing agreement with T-Mobile (such as TikTok), or from a non-clickable media such as Content Syndication or OOH.
Measurement_Metric	string	An indicator for the primary matched conversion metric to be used for performance analysis. Derived from Optimization metric from campaign taxonomy, or other markers in the record.
MDF	string	Manufacturer Device Fund - indicates if the record has been flagged as promoting a specific manufacturer initiative, such as iPhone, Samsung, Netflix, etc. Can support multiple MDFs in a single record, separated by comma. Derived from markers in the TMM Campaign Desc, and select values from Creative or Placement.
Partner	string	"The name of the Affiliate Partner
Affiliate Only"
Spend	double	The cost for the ad. May not include Fees or taxes.
Clicks	double	The number of users who clicked on the ad (only viable for clickable media) As determined by the source platform.
Impressions	double	The number of users who have been served an ad, and viewed it. As determined by the source platform. For Linear TV, this is the extrapolated Impression volume, and not the strict panel volume.
Panel_Impressions	double	The number of panel members who have been served an ad, and viewed it. As determined by iSpot. Linear and Advanced TV (OTT) only.
BizIL_Form_Fillout	bigint	Web event attributed (last touch) to served advertisement from the platform. See the Site Metrics Directory SharePoint doc for metric definition details.
BizIL_Prospect_Quality_Traffic	bigint	Web event attributed (last touch) to served advertisement from the platform. See the Site Metrics Directory SharePoint doc for metric definition details.
Broadband_Invoca_Order	bigint	Web event attributed (last touch) to served advertisement from the platform. See the Site Metrics Directory SharePoint doc for metric definition details.

Broadband_Eligibility_Check_Successful	bigint	Web event attributed (last touch) to served advertisement from the platform. See the Site Metrics Directory SharePoint doc for metric definition details.

DESCRIBE TABLE prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot;
output:
col_name	data_type	comment
view_source	string	The Lab42 Media Analytics view where the source record can be found. For the Integrated view, some records are limited from the source views to ensure deduplication of records (such as OTT is from iSpot, and the Display record is suppressed).
Date	date	Date of the customer engagement (Impressions / Click), or matched conversion (Meta only). Most platforms report on matched conversions as per the date of the click or impression, while Meta reflects the date of the conversion, not aligned to the engagement date.
Week_Start_Mon	date	The date of the start of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Week_End_Sun	date	The date of the end of the week, with a Monday through Sunday week (which aligns to media buying windows).
Agency	string	The name of the Agency who activated the record.
Brand	string	The T-Mobile Brand. A high level categorization of Postpaid, Prepaid, TFB, Broadband, Financial Services, etc. Not Specified is where the details within the record do not have enough indicators to identify a Brand. Other falls outside of the primary Brand we track (organic social or other specialized services).
LOB	string	T-Mobile Line of Business that falls under a LOB, such as Metro, T-Mobile Prepaid, Beyond the Smartphone (BTS), etc. Not Specified are missing enough indicators to assign an LOB. Other falls outside of standard LOBs.
Account_ID	string	The unique account id as it exists on the source platform.
Account_Name	string	The name of the account, as it exists on the source platform.
Account_Type	string	The type of the account (Microsoft, Google, etc.). Used exclusively for Paid Search.
Campaign_ID	string	The unique campaign ID, as it exists on the source platform. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most purposes, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Campaign_Name	string	The campaign name, as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. Note that campaign designation is a budgetary object related to how the activation was placed, and does not reflect the messaging campaign inside it. For most conversations, the term 'campaign' is better reflected by TMM_Campaign_Desc.
Campaign_Year	string	The year of the campaign (related to campaign id, not messaging campaign), as it appears in the taxonomy.
Placement_ID	string	Unique identifier for the placement-level entity on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Placement_Name	string	The Placement Name as it exists on the source platform. Platform-specific structures (e.g., Placement, Ad Group, Ad Squad, Ad Set) are standardized into a consistent hierarchy level across Media Analytics datasets.
Creative_ID	string	The unique Creative ID as it exists on the source platform.
Creative_Name	string	The Creative Name as it exists on the source platform. Often reflecting the long-form taxonomy rich name. This name does not reflect a description of the asset, as it is a container at the creative level that can support multiple assets (such as carousel). This is the level that the messaging campaign exists at, but messaging campaign is better served via TMM Campaign Desc.
Search_Campaign_Category	string	A categorization for the Campaign as defined in Paid Search.
Budget_Source	string	Budget Source as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Budgeted_Product_Line	string	Budgeted Product Line as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Master_Brand	string	Master Brand as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
OEM_Funded	string	Original Equipment Manufacturer (OEM) Funded indicator as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Program	string	Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Sub_Program	string	Sub-Program as is provided by campaign taxonomy.
Primary_Language	string	Primary Language as is provided by campaign taxonomy. Note that there is a better field for the messaging language, derived from creative taxonomy or other record markers. See Creative_Language.
Channel_Abbr	string	An abbreviated code to reflect the detail-level Channel definition. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Name	string	The detail-level channel name that reflects the media channel for the record. (Online Video, OTT, Linear TV, Paid Social - LinkedIn, Paid Social - Snapchat, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Abbr	string	An abbreviated code to reflect a channel grouping. Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Channel_Group_Name	string	The Channel Group Name to reflect the media channel, but aggregated to logic grouping (such as Paid Social, Paid Search, Display, etc.) Determined on taxonomy details, or other markers within the source record.
Media_Tactic	string	The Media Tactic of the record. Derived from a combination from Channel assignment, and other considerations such as Agency for Display - Direct, and Display - Programmatic. Or designations of Paid Search based on the search type - such as Paid Search - Branded, Paid Search - Non-branded. Primarily used to align MA Integrated to MMM, PIE, T&L, and other media analytics resources for consistent data alignment.
Optimization_Metric	string	The engagement metric that the ad is intended to drive (mid-funnel, SEC, etc.). As per campaign taxonomy, and may not reflect what campaign configuration option was selected on the platform when the campaign was placed.
Buy_Type	string	The Buy Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Telco_Profile	string	Telco Profile as per campaign taxonomy. May not reflect actual campaign configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Buying_Platform	string	Buying Platform as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Data_Type	string	Data Type as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy.
Enterprise_Base	string	Enterprise or Base targeting as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Publisher	string	Publisher info as for display, placement taxonomy, iSpot TV details, or Affiliate Program. Not applicable for other channels.
Target_Age	string	Target Age audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Geo_Type	string	"Indicates the Geo Type, National or Local for the record. Derived from markers within the record such as campaign name, or other indicators.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Geo	string	"Geo-based audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Does not reflect where the users are when engaging with ads, but what regions were targeted."
Target_Occupation	string	Targeted Occupation audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy May not reflect actual placement configuration used on the platform at time of activation.
Test_and_Learn	string	Indicator if the row is apart of a Test and Learn conversion lift studied as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy. Not well populated, not a reliable indicator for Test and Learn participation.
Creative_Agency	string	Creative Agency as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Agency_Ad_Number	string	Creative Agency Ad Number as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Creative_Language	string	Creative Language as per creative / ad taxonomy, enhanced by more robust marker indicators within the creative, placement, or campaign details.
Creative_Version	string	Creative Version as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Duration	string	Duration of the ad (if video) as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Media_Format	string	Media format as per creative / ad taxonomy.
Funnel_Stage	string	The Funnel Stage the ad is intended to target. Based on sub program, optimization metric, or other indicators. For TFB, The Funnel Stage matches the Sub Program from campaign taxonomy.
Audience	string	Audience targeting per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy intended to define sub segments such as Military, 55+, etc.
Audience_Descriptor	string	The targeted audience, as per placement / ad group / ad set taxonomy
Benefitting_Segment	string	Benefitting Segment as per creative / ad taxonomy Intended to denote what lob / brand / product will benefit from the ad, regardless of what budget is came from (Master Brand or Budget Source).
Creative_Intent	string	An indicator as to what the creative asset was to message the customer. Often populated with funnel description (Awareness, consideration, etc.) or a program indicator (BOAT, BOWLING, DINNER, etc.).
Spend_Source	string	An indicator as to where the spend value was sourced from. Primarily used to identify which DSP provided the source data for the placement, spend data. Note that spend at a creative level is often derived from a placement-level spend, allocated to creative by weighted impressions.
TMM_Code	string	The TMM Campaign Code, primarily derived from creative name, but supplemented by searching in other fields including the destination URL.
TMM_Campaign_Desc	string	The Description of the TMM Messaging Campaign. The primary resource for identifying which messaging campaign the ad supports.  Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Category	string	A categorization of the TMM Code. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
TMM_Message	string	Categorization of TMM campaigns to designate device, service, or other intended offer designations. Sourced from the TMM source (Minitab for historic and Genie for current).
Site_Directory	string	A grouping of Site names, to a more aggregated level. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_ID	string	The ID associated with the Site. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager.
Site_Name	string	The name of the site the as appeared on. Display only. Sourced from campaign manager. Not comprehensive as some display is sourced from Omnicom direct buys, or via DSPs which may obfuscate the Site name.
Conversions_Flag	string	An indicator if the record is expected to have Matched Conversion volumes or not. Intended to be used for filtering results when analyzing ad performance that is not expected to have matched conversions. The lack of matched conversions is due to the record source not having a data sharing agreement with T-Mobile (such as TikTok), or from a non-clickable media such as Content Syndication or OOH.
Measurement_Metric	string	An indicator for the primary matched conversion metric to be used for performance analysis. Derived from Optimization metric from campaign taxonomy, or other markers in the record.
MDF	string	Manufacturer Device Fund - indicates if the record has been flagged as promoting a specific manufacturer initiative, such as iPhone, Samsung, Netflix, etc. Can support multiple MDFs in a single record, separated by comma. Derived from markers in the TMM Campaign Desc, and select values from Creative or Placement.
Partner	string	"The name of the Affiliate Partner
Affiliate Only"
Spend	double	The cost for the ad. May not include Fees or taxes.
Clicks	double	The number of users who clicked on the ad (only viable for clickable media) As determined by the source platform.
Impressions	double	The number of users who have been served an ad, and viewed it. As determined by the source platform. For Linear TV, this is the extrapolated Impression volume, and not the strict panel volume.
Panel_Impressions	double	The number of panel members who have been served an ad, and viewed it. As determined by iSpot. Linear and Advanced TV (OTT) only.

WITH params AS (
    SELECT
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 8) AS prior_week_end,
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 14) AS prior_week_start
)

SELECT
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end,

    MAX(s.Date) AS latest_date_available,

    DATEDIFF(
        current_date(),
        MAX(s.Date)
    ) AS data_lag_days,

    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
        WHEN s.Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN s.Date
    END) AS prior_week_days_present,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN s.Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN s.Spend
        ELSE 0
    END) AS prior_week_spend,

    CASE
        WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
                 WHEN s.Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
                 THEN s.Date
             END) = 7
        THEN 'PASS - all 7 days available'
        ELSE 'CHECK - prior week incomplete'
    END AS availability_status

FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot s
CROSS JOIN params p

WHERE s.Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

GROUP BY
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end;
output:
prior_week_start	prior_week_end	latest_date_available	data_lag_days	prior_week_days_present	prior_week_spend	availability_status
2026-08-30	2026-09-05	2026-09-08	3	7	4509328.535227995	PASS - all 7 days available





WITH params AS (
    SELECT
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 8) AS prior_week_end,
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 14) AS prior_week_start
)

SELECT
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end,

    MAX(src.date) AS latest_date_available,

    DATEDIFF(
        current_date(),
        MAX(src.date)
    ) AS data_lag_days,

    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
        WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN src.date
    END) AS prior_week_days_present,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN src.spend
        ELSE 0
    END) AS prior_week_spend,

    CASE
        WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
                 WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
                 THEN src.date
             END) = 7
        THEN 'PASS - all 7 days available'
        ELSE 'CHECK - prior week incomplete'
    END AS availability_status

FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr src
CROSS JOIN params p

GROUP BY
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end;

WITH params AS (
    SELECT
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 8) AS prior_week_end,
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 14) AS prior_week_start
)

SELECT
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end,

    MAX(src.date) AS latest_date_available,

    DATEDIFF(
        current_date(),
        MAX(src.date)
    ) AS data_lag_days,

    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
        WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN src.date
    END) AS prior_week_days_present,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN src.cost
        ELSE 0
    END) AS prior_week_cost,

    CASE
        WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
                 WHEN src.date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
                 THEN src.date
             END) = 7
        THEN 'PASS - all 7 days available'
        ELSE 'CHECK - prior week incomplete'
    END AS availability_status

FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.sdi_tbl_sa360_gold_campaign_daily src
CROSS JOIN params p

GROUP BY
    p.prior_week_start,
    p.prior_week_end;

WITH params AS (
    SELECT
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 8) AS prior_week_end,
        date_sub(next_day(current_date(), 'SUN'), 14) AS prior_week_start
),

social AS (
    SELECT
        CASE
            WHEN Channel_Name IN (
                'Paid Social - Facebook',
                'Paid Social - Instagram'
            ) THEN 'Meta'
            ELSE REPLACE(Channel_Name, 'Paid Social - ', '')
        END AS platform,

        Date,
        Spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
)

SELECT
    platform,

    MAX(Date) AS latest_date_available,

    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
        WHEN Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN Date
    END) AS days_present_prior_week,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
        THEN Spend
        ELSE 0
    END) AS prior_week_spend,

    CASE
        WHEN COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
                 WHEN Date BETWEEN p.prior_week_start AND p.prior_week_end
                 THEN Date
             END) = 7
        THEN 'PASS'
        ELSE 'CHECK'
    END AS status

FROM social
CROSS JOIN params p

GROUP BY platform
ORDER BY platform;
output:

Creator	2024-09-30	0	0	CHECK
Digital Sponsorship	2026-03-30	0	0	CHECK

Indirect	2026-09-08	7	176925.34893796107	PASS

SELECT
    view_source,
    MAX(Date) AS latest_date_available,
    COUNT(*) AS rows_on_latest_date,
    SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN Date = current_date() - INTERVAL 1 DAY
            THEN Spend
            ELSE 0
        END
    ) AS yesterday_spend
FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
GROUP BY view_source
ORDER BY latest_date_available, view_source;

output:

media_analytics_xtwitter	2026-09-06	200746	0
media_analytics_linkedin	2026-09-07	236465	0
media_analytics_snapchat	2026-09-07	230016	0
media_analytics_ini_direct_social	2026-09-08	17987	0
media_analytics_meta	2026-09-08	3104477	0
media_analytics_pinterest	2026-09-08	568548	0
media_analytics_tiktok	2026-09-08	269928	0








-- =========================================================
-- HISTORICAL READINESS TEST
-- Monday Sep 7, 2026 @ 8:00 AM ET = 12:00 UTC
-- Prior reporting week = Aug 30 through Sep 5
-- =========================================================


-- 1. PROGRAMMATIC
SELECT
    'Programmatic' AS channel,
    MAX(date) AS latest_date_available,
    COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
        WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
        THEN date
    END) AS prior_week_days_present,
    SUM(CASE
        WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
        THEN spend
        ELSE 0
    END) AS prior_week_spend
FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T12:00:00Z';

WITH final_value AS (
    SELECT SUM(spend) AS final_spend
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    WHERE date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
),

snapshots AS (

    SELECT 'Mon 8 AM ET' AS checkpoint,
           MAX(date) AS latest_date,
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
               WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
               THEN date END) AS days_present,
           SUM(CASE
               WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
               THEN spend ELSE 0 END) AS snapshot_spend
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T12:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Mon 10 AM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T14:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Mon 12 PM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T16:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Mon 2 PM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T18:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Mon 4 PM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-07T20:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Tue 8 AM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-08T12:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Tue 10 AM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-08T14:00:00Z'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT 'Tue 12 PM ET',
           MAX(date),
           COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN date END),
           SUM(CASE WHEN date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prd_dbi_analytics.improvado.pbi_programmatic_browsers_currentyr
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-08T16:00:00Z'
)

SELECT
    checkpoint,
    latest_date,
    days_present,
    snapshot_spend,
    final_spend,
    ROUND(100 * snapshot_spend / final_spend, 2) AS pct_of_final,
    CASE
        WHEN days_present = 7
         AND snapshot_spend / final_spend >= 0.99
        THEN 'READY'
        ELSE 'NOT READY'
    END AS readiness_status
FROM snapshots
CROSS JOIN final_value;
output:

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-03T10:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
)

SELECT
    checkpoint,
    latest_date,
    days_present,
    snapshot_spend,
    final_spend,
    ROUND(100 * snapshot_spend / final_spend, 2) AS pct_of_final,
    CASE
        WHEN days_present = 7
         AND snapshot_spend / final_spend >= 0.99
        THEN 'READY'
        ELSE 'NOT READY'
    END AS readiness_status
FROM snapshots
CROSS JOIN final_value;


output:
checkpoint	latest_date	days_present	snapshot_spend	final_spend	pct_of_final	readiness_status
Mon 10 AM ET	2026-08-25	3	1615088.9027421745	3991105.053756975	40.47	NOT READY
Tue 10 AM ET	2026-08-30	7	3816847.6555611957	3991105.053756975	95.63	NOT READY
Wed 10 AM ET	2026-08-30	7	3816847.6555611957	3991105.053756975	95.63	NOT READY
Thu 6 AM ET	2026-09-01	7	3938604.868291435	3991105.053756975	98.68	NOT READY




-- ============================================================
-- PAID SOCIAL - MULTI-WEEK HISTORICAL READINESS
--
-- READY =
--   1) All calendar dates for the overall Paid Social week exist
--   2) Snapshot spend >= 99% of eventual settled spend
--
-- Checkpoints:
--   Monday 10 AM ET
--   Tuesday 10 AM ET
--   Wednesday 10 AM ET
--   Thursday 6 AM ET
-- ============================================================

WITH final_values AS (

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-15' AS week_end,
        SUM(Spend) AS final_spend
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-22',
        SUM(Spend)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-29',
        SUM(Spend)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-09-05',
        SUM(Spend)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05'
),

snapshots AS (

-- ============================================================
-- WEEK ENDING AUG 15
-- ============================================================

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-15' AS week_end,
        'Mon 10 AM ET' AS checkpoint,
        MAX(CASE
            WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15'
            THEN Date END) AS latest_week_date,
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE
            WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15'
            THEN Date END) AS days_present,
        SUM(CASE
            WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15'
            THEN Spend ELSE 0 END) AS snapshot_spend
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-17T14:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-15',
        'Tue 10 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-18T14:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-15',
        'Wed 10 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-19T14:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-15',
        'Thu 6 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-09' AND '2026-08-15' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-20T10:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'


-- ============================================================
-- WEEK ENDING AUG 22
-- ============================================================

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-22',
        'Mon 10 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-26T14:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-22',
        'Thu 6 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-16' AND '2026-08-22' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-08-27T10:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

    UNION ALL

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-08-29',

        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29' THEN Date END),

    SELECT
        DATE '2026-09-05',
        'Thu 6 AM ET',
        MAX(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN Date END),
        COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN Date END),
        SUM(CASE WHEN Date BETWEEN '2026-08-30' AND '2026-09-05' THEN Spend ELSE 0 END)
    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-10T10:00:00Z'
    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'

SELECT
    s.week_end,
    s.checkpoint,
    s.latest_week_date,
    s.days_present,
    ROUND(s.snapshot_spend, 2) AS snapshot_spend,
    ROUND(f.final_spend, 2) AS final_spend,
    ROUND(100 * s.snapshot_spend / f.final_spend, 2) AS pct_of_final,

    CASE
        WHEN s.days_present = 7
         AND s.snapshot_spend / f.final_spend >= 0.99
        THEN 'READY'
        ELSE 'NOT READY'
    END AS readiness_status

FROM snapshots s
LEFT JOIN final_values f
    ON s.week_end = f.week_end

ORDER BY
    s.week_end,
    CASE s.checkpoint
        WHEN 'Mon 10 AM ET' THEN 1
        WHEN 'Tue 10 AM ET' THEN 2
        WHEN 'Wed 10 AM ET' THEN 3
        WHEN 'Thu 6 AM ET'  THEN 4
    END;


-- ============================================================
-- PAID SOCIAL PLATFORM-LEVEL BACKFILL
-- Week: Aug 23 - Aug 29
--
-- Important:
-- We compare snapshot days WITH ROWS against final days WITH ROWS.
-- We do NOT assume every platform must have spend all 7 days.
-- ============================================================

WITH final_platform AS (

    SELECT
        CASE
            WHEN Channel_Name IN (
                'Paid Social - Facebook',
                'Paid Social - Instagram'
            ) THEN 'Meta'

            WHEN Channel_Name = 'Paid Social - Twitter'
            THEN 'X / Twitter'

            ELSE REPLACE(Channel_Name, 'Paid Social - ', '')
        END AS platform,

        COUNT(DISTINCT Date) AS final_days_with_rows,
        SUM(Spend) AS final_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY 1
),

tuesday_snapshot AS (

    SELECT
        CASE
            WHEN Channel_Name IN (
                'Paid Social - Facebook',
                'Paid Social - Instagram'
            ) THEN 'Meta'

            WHEN Channel_Name = 'Paid Social - Twitter'
            THEN 'X / Twitter'

            ELSE REPLACE(Channel_Name, 'Paid Social - ', '')
        END AS platform,

        COUNT(DISTINCT Date) AS tue_days_with_rows,
        SUM(Spend) AS tue_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-01T14:00:00Z'

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY 1
),

thursday_snapshot AS (

    SELECT
        CASE
            WHEN Channel_Name IN (
                'Paid Social - Facebook',
                'Paid Social - Instagram'
            ) THEN 'Meta'

            WHEN Channel_Name = 'Paid Social - Twitter'
            THEN 'X / Twitter'

            ELSE REPLACE(Channel_Name, 'Paid Social - ', '')
        END AS platform,

        COUNT(DISTINCT Date) AS thu_days_with_rows,
        SUM(Spend) AS thu_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-03T10:00:00Z'

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY 1
),

platforms AS (

    SELECT platform FROM final_platform
    UNION
    SELECT platform FROM tuesday_snapshot
    UNION
    SELECT platform FROM thursday_snapshot
)

SELECT
    p.platform,

    f.final_days_with_rows,

    t.tue_days_with_rows,
    ROUND(t.tue_spend, 2) AS tue_spend,
    ROUND(f.final_spend, 2) AS final_spend,

    ROUND(
        100 * COALESCE(t.tue_spend,0)
        / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS tue_pct_of_final,

    h.thu_days_with_rows,
    ROUND(h.thu_spend, 2) AS thu_spend,

    ROUND(
        100 * COALESCE(h.thu_spend,0)
        / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS thu_pct_of_final,

    CASE
        WHEN COALESCE(t.tue_days_with_rows,0) = f.final_days_with_rows
         AND COALESCE(t.tue_spend,0) / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0) >= 0.99
        THEN 'READY'
        ELSE 'NOT READY'
    END AS tue_status,

    CASE
        WHEN COALESCE(h.thu_days_with_rows,0) = f.final_days_with_rows
         AND COALESCE(h.thu_spend,0) / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0) >= 0.99
        THEN 'READY'
        ELSE 'NOT READY'
    END AS thu_status

FROM platforms p

LEFT JOIN final_platform f
    ON p.platform = f.platform

LEFT JOIN tuesday_snapshot t
    ON p.platform = t.platform

LEFT JOIN thursday_snapshot h
    ON p.platform = h.platform

WHERE COALESCE(f.final_spend,0) <> 0

ORDER BY
    tue_pct_of_final ASC,
    p.platform;


-- ============================================================
-- PAID SOCIAL DAY-LEVEL BACKFILL
-- Week: Aug 23 - Aug 29
--
-- Compare:
-- Tuesday 10 AM
-- Thursday 6 AM
-- Final settled spend
-- ============================================================

WITH final_day AS (

    SELECT
        Date,
        SUM(Spend) AS final_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY Date
),

tuesday_day AS (

    SELECT
        Date,
        SUM(Spend) AS tue_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-01T14:00:00Z'

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY Date
),

thursday_day AS (

    SELECT
        Date,
        SUM(Spend) AS thu_spend

    FROM prdrzranalytics.lab42.media_analytics_integrated_snapshot
    TIMESTAMP AS OF '2026-09-03T10:00:00Z'

    WHERE Channel_Group_Name = 'Paid Social'
      AND Date BETWEEN '2026-08-23' AND '2026-08-29'

    GROUP BY Date
)

SELECT
    f.Date,

    ROUND(COALESCE(t.tue_spend,0),2) AS tue_10am_spend,

    ROUND(f.final_spend,2) AS final_spend,

    ROUND(
        100 * COALESCE(t.tue_spend,0)
        / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS tue_pct_of_final,

    ROUND(COALESCE(h.thu_spend,0),2) AS thu_6am_spend,

    ROUND(
        100 * COALESCE(h.thu_spend,0)
        / NULLIF(f.final_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS thu_pct_of_final,

    ROUND(
        f.final_spend - COALESCE(t.tue_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS missing_on_tuesday,

    ROUND(
        f.final_spend - COALESCE(h.thu_spend,0),
        2
    ) AS missing_on_thursday

FROM final_day f

LEFT JOIN tuesday_day t
    ON f.Date = t.Date

LEFT JOIN thursday_day h
    ON f.Date = h.Date

ORDER BY f.Date;
